# HB-OS-cubook - the CU capsule contract layer (`src/cubook`, `cmd/srf-cu-*`)

Hybrid-format companion book (`hb-hybrid/v1`, book id `HB-OSCUBOOK-001`) written from the Go code at OpenSuperficies HEAD 07d789b. Code is truth: where a comment and the code disagree the book states the code and lists the disagreement in the section 7 findings. Every number is tagged `[MEASURED, <receipt>]`, `[CITED, <coordinate>]` or `[TARGET]`; hb-s8..hb-s10 are generated by `hyperbooks/_tools/os/hb_os_driver.py` and never edited by hand. References have the form `path:L0-L1 sha_ast:<hex>` and are re-checked against the live AST by the drift gate.

## <a id="hb-s1"></a><a id="pm-s1"></a>1. Thesis and non-claims

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s1","claims":[],"code_anchors":["src/cubook/rc/rc.go::Code","src/cubook/prodgate/prodgate.go::Check"],"diataxis":"explanation","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"thesis","n":1,"purpose":"What this book asserts about the cubook contract layer and what it refuses to assert.","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"Thesis and non-claims"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
The contract layer is small on purpose: one exit-code table (`rc`), three frozen wire schemas with strict decoders (`action`), one append-only step log (`ajson`), one production door (`prodgate`), a RAM-only capture path (`capture`), a pure safety gate with an executor (`exec`), and an MCP surface (`mcp`). The four binaries `srf-cu-exec`, `srf-cu-mcp`, `srf-cu-snap` and `srf-cu-wlcap` only parse flags and call those packages.

**Claims.** (1) Exit codes are 0 and 2..7 only. (2) A binary reaches the real X server only through `prodgate.Check`. (3) Wire documents are integer-only and closed. (4) Frames live only under the ramws root. (5) A refused action emits zero input events.

**Non-claims.** The book does not claim the behaviour of the X server, the capsule image, the vision engine (`vision/chunk_analyze.py`) or the host janitor; it does not measure performance; it does not replace the tests, which are the oracles listed in section 7. The 86 Go files (71 under src/cubook and 15 under cmd/srf-cu-*) [MEASURED, goextract census over HEAD] are described by their live AST, and a statement here that no symbol backs is a defect of the book, not of the code.

### <a id="hb-s3.rc"></a>rc: the shared exit-code table

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s3.rc","claims":[],"code_anchors":[],"diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"package","n":3,"purpose":"rc holds the single exit-code table every srf-cu-* binary shares (SPEC_INT R4: codes 0 and 2..7, the value 1 is never produced). The constants are OK 0, Usage 2, Anchor 3, Stale 4, Gate 5, Budget 6 and Human 7 (src/cubook/rc/rc.go:9-9 sha_ast:63363416e7e4). A table name (E_GATE, E_SCHEMA, ...) maps to one code; Code sends an unknown name to Gate (5) and Of sends a nil error to 0, a wrapped *Error to its table code and any other error to Usage (2).","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"rc: the shared exit-code table"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
`rc` holds the single exit-code table every `srf-cu-*` binary shares (SPEC_INT R4: codes 0 and 2..7, the value 1 is never produced). The constants are OK 0, Usage 2, Anchor 3, Stale 4, Gate 5, Budget 6 and Human 7 (`src/cubook/rc/rc.go:9-9 sha_ast:63363416e7e4`). A table name (`E_GATE`, `E_SCHEMA`, ...) maps to one code; `Code` sends an unknown name to Gate (5) and `Of` sends a nil error to 0, a wrapped `*Error` to its table code and any other error to Usage (2).

```
# PM-P3-HEADER: src/cubook/rc/rc.go::Of
# INPUTS: err error (any value, nil allowed)
# PRECONDITIONS: none; a wrapped *rc.Error is found with errors.As
# OUTPUTS: int exit code: 0 for nil, Code(Name) for a wrapped *Error, 2 (Usage) for any other error
# TIME: O(depth of the wrap chain) plus one map lookup
# SPACE: O(1)
# ARITHMETIC: integer constants only; no float
# FAILURE: never panics and never returns 1; an unknown table name maps to Gate (5) inside Code
```

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.inv.v2 -->

Exported: `Code` (`src/cubook/rc/rc.go:60-65 sha_ast:54a8b1c3a746`): Code maps a table name to its rc; unknown names map to Gate (5).; `Error` (`src/cubook/rc/rc.go:49-52 sha_ast:9cee4e6e326e`): Error carries a table name; its exit code is table[Name].; `New` (`src/cubook/rc/rc.go:57-57 sha_ast:09a6ef4c904d`): New builds an *Error.; `Of` (`src/cubook/rc/rc.go:68-77 sha_ast:afcdc2bdddda`): Of maps any error to an rc: nil -> 0, *Error -> table, anything else -> 2 (E_IO).; `Table` (`src/cubook/rc/rc.go:40-46 sha_ast:d5b4920e1565`): Table returns a copy of the name -> rc table.


### <a id="hb-s3.action"></a>action: wire types and strict decoders

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s3.action","claims":[],"code_anchors":[],"diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"package","n":3,"purpose":"action defines the three frozen wire schemas vqa.action/v1, vqa.act-plan/v1 and vqa.act-step/v1 (constants src/cubook/action/types.go:6-6 sha_ast:71c6f41fb987) and their strict decoders. Decoding rejects any non-integer number (rejectFloats), refuses unknown fields (DisallowUnknownFields) and then validates closed enums and bounds; a decode failure is an E_SCHEMA error, rc 2. ROI is either the string \"target\" or a four-integer rectangle. Canonical gives the key-sorted compact JSON and Action.SHA is the sha256 over it, the digest L","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"action: wire types and strict decoders"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
`action` defines the three frozen wire schemas `vqa.action/v1`, `vqa.act-plan/v1` and `vqa.act-step/v1` (constants `src/cubook/action/types.go:6-6 sha_ast:71c6f41fb987`) and their strict decoders. Decoding rejects any non-integer number (`rejectFloats`), refuses unknown fields (`DisallowUnknownFields`) and then validates closed enums and bounds; a decode failure is an `E_SCHEMA` error, rc 2. `ROI` is either the string `"target"` or a four-integer rectangle. `Canonical` gives the key-sorted compact JSON and `Action.SHA` is the sha256 over it, the digest L2 and L3 agree on.

```
# PM-P3-HEADER: src/cubook/action/decode.go::DecodeAction
# INPUTS: data []byte, one raw vqa.action/v1 JSON document
# PRECONDITIONS: none; the bytes need not be valid JSON, the decoder reports it
# OUTPUTS: *Action that passed Validate, or a schema error
# TIME: O(len(data)): one float walk, one decode, one validation
# SPACE: O(len(data)) for the decoded tree
# ARITHMETIC: integer only: rejectFloats refuses any non-integer number before decoding
# FAILURE: non-integer number, unknown field, malformed JSON or failed Validate returns an E_SCHEMA error (rc 2); no partial action is returned
```

### <a id="hb-s3.ajson"></a>ajson: the append-only act_steps.jsonl writer

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s3.ajson","claims":[],"code_anchors":[],"diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"package","n":3,"purpose":"ajson is the append-only act_steps.jsonl writer. Open takes flock(LOCK_EX|LOCK_NB) so exactly one process writes; a second holder gets an E_GATE error (rc 5, see finding F-B01). On open a torn final line (unterminated trailing bytes) is truncated and counted in Recovered; a bad or non-increasing line anywhere else is E_SCHEMA and the file is left untouched. Append validates the step through action.DecodeStep, requires strictly increasing step numbers, writes one line and calls the syncFile seam (fsync) before returning.","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"ajson: the append-only act_steps.jsonl writer"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
`ajson` is the append-only `act_steps.jsonl` writer. `Open` takes `flock(LOCK_EX|LOCK_NB)` so exactly one process writes; a second holder gets an `E_GATE` error (rc 5, see finding F-B01). On open a torn final line (unterminated trailing bytes) is truncated and counted in `Recovered`; a bad or non-increasing line anywhere else is `E_SCHEMA` and the file is left untouched. `Append` validates the step through `action.DecodeStep`, requires strictly increasing step numbers, writes one line and calls the `syncFile` seam (fsync) before returning.

```
# PM-P3-HEADER: src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::Open
# INPUTS: path string of the act_steps.jsonl file
# PRECONDITIONS: the process may create the file with mode 0o600; no other process holds its flock
# OUTPUTS: *Writer positioned after the last complete line, Recovered set to the bytes truncated from a torn tail
# TIME: O(file size): the file is read whole and every line decoded
# SPACE: O(file size) for the read buffer
# ARITHMETIC: integers only: uint32 step comparison and int64 byte counts
# FAILURE: lock held returns E_GATE (rc 5); an IO error returns E_IO (rc 2); a corrupt mid-file line or a non-increasing step returns E_SCHEMA (rc 2) and the file is not rewritten
```

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.inv.v2 -->
```json
{"au":[{"id":"AU-AJSON-SECOND","symbols":["src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::Open"],"text":"Do not open act_steps.jsonl from a second process while a writer is alive and do not retry in a loop; the lock is non-blocking by design and the refusal is the contract."}],"inv":[{"id":"INV-AJSON-LOCK","symbols":["src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::Open"],"text":"Open holds a non-blocking exclusive flock on the file; a second Open while the first writer lives fails with rc 5 instead of waiting."},{"id":"INV-AJSON-TORN","symbols":["src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::Writer.recover"],"text":"recover truncates only an unterminated final line; every complete line must decode as a vqa.act-step/v1 or the open fails with E_SCHEMA."},{"id":"INV-AJSON-ORDER","symbols":["src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::Writer.advance","src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::Writer.Append","src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::ReadAll"],"text":"Step numbers on disk are strictly increasing; advance and Append refuse an equal or smaller step with E_SCHEMA."},{"id":"INV-AJSON-OPEN","symbols":["src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::Open"],"text":"Open: Open locks (non-blocking) and repairs the file; a second holder gets E_RAM_LOCKED-class rc 5. (code: E_GATE, see s7)"},{"id":"INV-AJSON-READALL","symbols":["src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::ReadAll"],"text":"ReadAll: ReadAll returns the decoded steps of a jsonl file (every line must be valid)."},{"id":"INV-AJSON-WRITER","symbols":["src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::Writer"],"text":"Writer (class) has the code signature Writer struct { f *os.File last uint32 have bool Recovered int64 }"},{"id":"INV-AJSON-WRITER-APPEND","symbols":["src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::Writer.Append"],"text":"Writer.Append: Append writes one step line (must carry action_sha256 and input_sha256) and fsyncs."},{"id":"INV-AJSON-WRITER-CLOSE","symbols":["src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::Writer.Close"],"text":"Writer.Close: Close releases the lock; idempotent."},{"id":"INV-AJSON-WRITER-ADVANCE","symbols":["src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::Writer.advance"],"text":"Writer.advance: advance enforces strictly increasing step numbers."},{"id":"INV-AJSON-WRITER-RECOVER","symbols":["src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::Writer.recover"],"text":"Writer.recover: recover repairs only a torn FINAL line (unterminated trailing bytes)."},{"id":"INV-AJSON-SYNCFILE","symbols":["src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go::syncFile"],"text":"syncFile: syncFile is the durability barrier after every append (a test seam)."}],"module":"src/cubook/ajson"}
```

### <a id="hb-s3.prodgate"></a>prodgate: the production gate

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s3.prodgate","claims":[],"code_anchors":[],"diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"package","n":3,"purpose":"prodgate decides whether a CU binary may drive the real X server. Check reads only the variables it needs: OS_CU_TEST=1 yields TestFake first and nothing else is read; otherwise the capsule markers, DISPLAY equal to :100 and a 32-hex-digit VQA_ISO equal to the root-window isolation id must all hold, tested in that order so a host never reaches the X server. Every refusal is an E_GATE error (rc 5) with the reason not_in_capsule, host_display or iso_mismatch. Build is the single production door: Check first, then the XTest actuato","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"prodgate: the production gate"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
`prodgate` decides whether a CU binary may drive the real X server. `Check` reads only the variables it needs: `OS_CU_TEST=1` yields `TestFake` first and nothing else is read; otherwise the capsule markers, `DISPLAY` equal to `:100` and a 32-hex-digit `VQA_ISO` equal to the root-window isolation id must all hold, tested in that order so a host never reaches the X server. Every refusal is an `E_GATE` error (rc 5) with the reason `not_in_capsule`, `host_display` or `iso_mismatch`. `Build` is the single production door: `Check` first, then the XTest actuator and the Snapper observer.

```
# PM-P3-HEADER: src/cubook/prodgate/prodgate.go::Check
# INPUTS: env func(string) string, probe capture.CapsuleProbe, iso IsoReader
# PRECONDITIONS: env closes over the process environment; iso may be nil only when OS_CU_TEST is 1
# OUTPUTS: (Mode, error): TestFake for OS_CU_TEST=1; Prod only when capsule, DISPLAY :100 and VQA_ISO equal to the root-window id all hold; the zero Mode on refusal
# TIME: O(1) plus one probe and one iso read
# SPACE: O(1)
# ARITHMETIC: none: string comparison and the regexp ^[0-9a-f]{32}$
# FAILURE: refusal is an E_GATE error (rc 5) with reason not_in_capsule, host_display or iso_mismatch; an iso read error is iso_mismatch; zero input events are emitted on refusal
```

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.inv.v2 -->

### <a id="hb-s4.capture"></a>capture: the OS-side raw RGB24 capture path

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s4.capture","claims":[],"code_anchors":[],"diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"package","n":4,"purpose":"capture is the OS-side raw RGB24 capture path of the CU capsule (os-cu-0 L1). Frames go only into ramws paths the caller hands in and evidence JSON is path-free. Errors are one type, Error{Class, Reason}; the command maps Class through rc. PathGuard refuses any frame path that is not under DefaultRamRoot on a tmpfs/ramfs mount, CheckOut refuses an evidence directory under /dev/shm, GuardDisplay allows exactly :100 inside the capsule and GuardIso compares the root-window isolation id with VQA_ISO. FrameSource implementations read","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"capture: the OS-side raw RGB24 capture path"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
`capture` is the OS-side raw RGB24 capture path of the CU capsule (os-cu-0 L1). Frames go only into ramws paths the caller hands in and evidence JSON is path-free. Errors are one type, `Error{Class, Reason}`; the command maps `Class` through `rc`. `PathGuard` refuses any frame path that is not under `DefaultRamRoot` on a tmpfs/ramfs mount, `CheckOut` refuses an evidence directory under `/dev/shm`, `GuardDisplay` allows exactly `:100` inside the capsule and `GuardIso` compares the root-window isolation id with `VQA_ISO`. `FrameSource` implementations read an XWD framebuffer by `ReaderAt` (`XWDSource`), `mmap` (`MmapSource`) or injected MIT-SHM (`XshmSource`); `OpenSource` accepts the closed enum readat|mmap|xshm. `Ring` keeps 4 resident RGB24 frames in `frames.raw` (mode `FileMode`, no world bits). `Pacer` is the fixed-step accumulator behind `Run`; `FrameNs` is 16,666,667 ns [CITED `src/cubook/capture/livedriver.go:26-26 sha_ast:7254fe3da1b0`]. `Run` captures render slots in chunks, logs gaps and never back-fills them; conservation is Published = Frames + Overrun + Dropped. `Analyzer` and `HostAnalyzer` are the per-chunk analysis hooks; host markers are opened with `O_NOFOLLOW`. `RunArgs` builds the docker run vector of a capsule slot and refuses anything that could weaken isolation; `Manifest`, `Capsule` and `Receipt` are the closed `vqa.frame/v1`, `vqa.capsule/v1` and `os.cu.capture-receipt/v1` documents.

```
# PM-P3-HEADER: src/cubook/capture/livedriver.go::Pacer.Tick
# INPUTS: nowNs int64, a monotonic clock sample in nanoseconds
# PRECONDITIONS: the pacer step delta is in (0, maxDeltaNs]; otherwise no step is ever due
# OUTPUTS: (idx, missing, ok): the first call returns idx 0 and ok; later calls return the render_index and the steps skipped before it; ok is false when no step is due
# TIME: O(1)
# SPACE: O(1) in the Pacer struct
# ARITHMETIC: int64 integer division and modulo only; an elapsed span is clamped to 1<<62 and a clock that does not advance adds zero; no float
# FAILURE: an invalid delta never produces a step; a backwards clock is clamped to zero elapsed; never panics
```

### <a id="hb-s4.capture-analyzer"></a>`src/cubook/capture/analyzer.go`

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s4.capture-analyzer","claims":[],"code_anchors":[],"covers":"src/cubook/capture/analyzer.go","diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"file","n":4,"purpose":"src/cubook/capture/analyzer.go defines AnalyzeFailedFile, AnalyzeRunner, AnalyzeTotals, AnalyzedFile, Analyzer, DefaultHostWait, ExecRunner, GridFor.","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"src/cubook/capture/analyzer.go"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
Exported: `AnalyzeRunner` (`src/cubook/capture/analyzer.go:49-49 sha_ast:2f4e057e17fd`): AnalyzeRunner runs the engine on one chunk and returns the stdout of vision/chunk_analyze.py.; `AnalyzeTotals` (`src/cubook/capture/analyzer.go:97-101 sha_ast:973fdc576353`): AnalyzeTotals are the 64-bit accumulators over all analyzed chunks.; `Analyzer` (`src/cubook/capture/analyzer.go:104-112 sha_ast:6bbfddac963b`): Analyzer implements ChunkHook for one run geometry.; `Analyzer.Analyze` (`src/cubook/capture/analyzer.go:126-174 sha_ast:ab199743b121`): Analyze runs the engine on chunkDir and fills row.; `Analyzer.Hook` (`src/cubook/capture/analyzer.go:115-115 sha_ast:e7d7b9f7160c`): Hook is the ChunkHook P wires into closeChunk.; `Analyzer.Totals` (`src/cubook/capture/analyzer.go:118-122 sha_ast:aa60e979a645`): Totals returns a snapshot of the 64-bit accumulators.

### <a id="hb-s4.capture-fbsrc"></a>`src/cubook/capture/fbsrc.go`

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s4.capture-fbsrc","claims":[],"code_anchors":[],"covers":"src/cubook/capture/fbsrc.go","diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"file","n":4,"purpose":"src/cubook/capture/fbsrc.go defines MmapSource, NewMmapSource, NewXshmSource, OpenSource, ShmGetter, XshmSource.","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"src/cubook/capture/fbsrc.go"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
Exported: `MmapSource` (`src/cubook/capture/fbsrc.go:20-26 sha_ast:5b50b3ee6a91`): MmapSource maps the framebuffer file read-only once; Frame is a single forward pass over the mapping with no syscall and no intermediate scratch copy (the paylo; `MmapSource.Close` (`src/cubook/capture/fbsrc.go:82-89 sha_ast:5921e79ede77`): Close unmaps; Frame after Close fails instead of faulting.; `MmapSource.Frame` (`src/cubook/capture/fbsrc.go:59-79 sha_ast:3f840e357d7a`): Frame converts the mapped BGRX pixels straight into dst (RGB24); the row slices are hoisted per row.; `MmapSource.Header` (`src/cubook/capture/fbsrc.go:56-56 sha_ast:b901f0100b85`): Header is the header cached at open (geometry of the map).; `NewMmapSource` (`src/cubook/capture/fbsrc.go:29-53 sha_ast:5835f639ba5c`): NewMmapSource maps f, parses the header once and proves the pixel range inside the mapping (hoisted bounds).; `OpenSource` (`src/cubook/capture/fbsrc.go:108-129 sha_ast:afd478883297`): OpenSource builds the FrameSource named by --src (closed enum readat/mmap/xshm).

## <a id="hb-s5"></a><a id="pm-s5"></a>5. Executor and MCP

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s5","claims":[],"code_anchors":[],"diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"reference","n":5,"purpose":"Section 5 of the cubook book: Executor and MCP.","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"Executor and MCP"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->

### <a id="hb-s5.exec"></a>exec: the L2 executor and its pure safety gate

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s5.exec","claims":[],"code_anchors":[],"diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"package","n":5,"purpose":"exec lowers vqa.action/v1 onto an Actuator, gates it with the pure pre-act function Check and derives the verdict from frames only (actuator rc never enters Verdict). Executor.Step runs one action and Executor.Run runs a plan to the first halting result. Check returns a zero Decision for go; any other Decision means zero input events. Its order is: base screen size or located frame stale (E_STALE, rc 4), pre.screen_is mismatch (screen_mismatch, rc 5), risk irrev (rc 7), risk write needs match level L0 or L1, the deny list, den","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"exec: the L2 executor and its pure safety gate"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
`exec` lowers `vqa.action/v1` onto an `Actuator`, gates it with the pure pre-act function `Check` and derives the verdict from frames only (actuator rc never enters `Verdict`). `Executor.Step` runs one action and `Executor.Run` runs a plan to the first halting result. `Check` returns a zero `Decision` for go; any other Decision means zero input events. Its order is: base screen size or located frame stale (`E_STALE`, rc 4), `pre.screen_is` mismatch (`screen_mismatch`, rc 5), risk `irrev` (rc 7), risk `write` needs match level L0 or L1, the deny list, denied key chords, typing into a terminal, and the stuck rule (three no-effect repeats of the same screen and action). `Replay` re-derives every recorded step from the plan and frames with no actuator. `XTestActuator` lowers onto XTEST and tracks held buttons and keys for `ReleaseAll`. The `WL*` types lower a prereg `os.cu.prereg-wl/v2` item (and v3 through lane-V hooks that fail closed) to timed events fired against a monotonic clock.

```
# PM-P3-HEADER: src/cubook/exec/safety.go::Check
# INPUTS: in SafetyIn: the action, located target, frame before, base size, deny list, mock flag, counters
# PRECONDITIONS: the caller supplies the located target and the frame observed before acting
# OUTPUTS: Decision{Verdict, Stop, RC}; the zero Decision means go, any other means zero input events
# TIME: O(number of deny rules) for the deny-list match, otherwise O(1)
# SPACE: O(1)
# ARITHMETIC: integer comparisons only (screen size, render index, counters); no float
# FAILURE: a stale screen is rc 4, a policy refusal rc 5, an irreversible action rc 7; the function has no side effects
```

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.inv.v2 -->

### <a id="hb-s5.exec-bounds"></a>`src/cubook/exec/bounds.go`

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s5.exec-bounds","claims":[],"code_anchors":[],"covers":"src/cubook/exec/bounds.go","diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"file","n":5,"purpose":"src/cubook/exec/bounds.go defines InRect, InScreen.","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"src/cubook/exec/bounds.go"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
Exported: `InRect` (`src/cubook/exec/bounds.go:22-28 sha_ast:8419a911d42b`): InRect reports that r = [x, y, rw, rh] is a non-empty rect inside the w x h screen: x+rw <= w and y+rh <= h.; `InScreen` (`src/cubook/exec/bounds.go:13-19 sha_ast:ae4259e2b5e9`): InScreen reports 0 <= x < w and 0 <= y < h for p = [x, y] (exactly two coordinates).

### <a id="hb-s5.mcp"></a>mcp: the MCP action surface and dispatcher

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s5.mcp","claims":[],"code_anchors":[],"diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"package","n":5,"purpose":"mcp is the MCP action surface of the CU layer: a stdio JSON-RPC server (Server.Serve) that turns each tool call into exactly one vqa.action/v1 (Server.build, strictFields refuses keys outside the tool property list), plus the downstream dispatcher. Dispatcher.Execute handles op mcp_call and delegates every other op to the GUI executor; verification never uses the MCP rc, only Verifier evidence. Registry is loaded strictly (unknown field or bad enum is rc 2). Guards: CheckEndpoint admits only the capsule forwarder at 127.0.0.1 ports 19","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"mcp: the MCP action surface and dispatcher"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
`mcp` is the MCP action surface of the CU layer: a stdio JSON-RPC server (`Server.Serve`) that turns each tool call into exactly one `vqa.action/v1` (`Server.build`, `strictFields` refuses keys outside the tool property list), plus the downstream dispatcher. `Dispatcher.Execute` handles op `mcp_call` and delegates every other op to the GUI executor; verification never uses the MCP rc, only `Verifier` evidence. `Registry` is loaded strictly (unknown field or bad enum is rc 2). Guards: `CheckEndpoint` admits only the capsule forwarder at 127.0.0.1 ports 19876..19883, `CheckEnvelope` fails when a 12-byte window of server content appears in the envelope (server bytes live only in the content-addressed `CAS`; over `MaxContent` is rc 6), `GuardGodotProject` allows only a clean absolute path inside the capsule scratch dir, and `ScanScript` statically denies unsafe Blender scripts. `ASTGate` runs the in-capsule AST check before a live script is dispatched; outside the capsule the script tools stay unwired (rc 5). `Unwired` refuses every GUI action and `MockGUI` is test-only and reports UNVERIFIED.

```
# PM-P3-HEADER: src/cubook/mcp/guard.go::CheckEndpoint
# INPUTS: ep string, an MCP endpoint, optionally with an http or tcp scheme prefix and a path suffix
# PRECONDITIONS: none; a malformed string is refused, not trusted
# OUTPUTS: nil only for 127.0.0.1 with a port in 19876..19883; otherwise a gate error
# TIME: O(len(ep))
# SPACE: O(1)
# ARITHMETIC: integer port comparison only; ports 9876, 9877 and 8770 are named operator sessions and refused
# FAILURE: endpoint_not_forwarder for a non-loopback host, a port outside 19876..19883 or an unparsable endpoint; operator_session for 9876, 9877 and 8770; both are gate errors (rc 5)
```

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.inv.v2 -->

### <a id="hb-s5.mcp-astgate"></a>`src/cubook/mcp/astgate.go`

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s5.mcp-astgate","claims":[],"code_anchors":[],"covers":"src/cubook/mcp/astgate.go","diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"file","n":5,"purpose":"src/cubook/mcp/astgate.go defines ASTChecker, ASTGate, ASTRunner, ExecRunner.","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"src/cubook/mcp/astgate.go"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
Exported: `ASTChecker` (`src/cubook/mcp/astgate.go:14-16 sha_ast:2b7efc7351b2`): ASTChecker is the in-capsule gate every live script must pass before it is dispatched (FX3 ruling): nil error = allowed, rc 5 gate error = refused.; `ASTGate` (`src/cubook/mcp/astgate.go:26-29 sha_ast:19e92461ecc6`): ASTGate is the ASTChecker over a Runner.; `ASTRunner` (`src/cubook/mcp/astgate.go:20-22 sha_ast:b14a1379d3fe`): ASTRunner runs capsule/astcheck/astcheck.py on a script and returns its rc and stdout.; `ExecRunner` (`src/cubook/mcp/astgate.go:60-64 sha_ast:cd01505080af`): ExecRunner runs the checker with the capsule's stock python3 in isolated mode.

### <a id="hb-s6.srf-cu-exec"></a>srf-cu-exec

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s6.srf-cu-exec","claims":[],"code_anchors":["cmd/srf-cu-exec/main.go::run"],"diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"binary","n":6,"purpose":"srf-cu-exec is validate / run (fake world) / replay vqa.act-plan/v1 plans, plus `run --prereg` for prereg-wl items (wl.go).","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"srf-cu-exec"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
`srf-cu-exec` is validate / run (fake world) / replay vqa.act-plan/v1 plans, plus `run --prereg` for prereg-wl items (wl.go). Usage: `validate --plan P | replay --plan P --steps S --frames F [--out O] | run --plan P --out S [--deny D] [--stop-file X] [--mock] (--fb F --ramws-dir D --iso I | OS_CU_TEST=1 --mock --frames F)`; no arguments or a missing --plan is usage, rc 2. `wl.go` holds `readStart`/`waitStart`, the actuator side of the START marker contract (open with O_NOFOLLOW, regular file with one link).

The module doc and flags are in `cmd/srf-cu-exec/main.go`; its `run` is `cmd/srf-cu-exec/main.go:41-168 sha_ast:9e477eb16306`.

```
# PM-P3-HEADER: cmd/srf-cu-exec/main.go::run
# INPUTS: args []string, the subcommand and its flags; stdout and stderr writers
# PRECONDITIONS: args[0] is validate, replay or run; run with --prereg takes the wl path
# OUTPUTS: int exit code: 0 on success, rc.Usage (2) for no arguments, an unparsable flag or a missing --plan, otherwise rc.Of of the error
# TIME: dominated by plan and frames I/O
# SPACE: O(plan size)
# ARITHMETIC: integer codes from src/cubook/rc; --start-wait-ms is an int capped by maxStartWaitMS
# FAILURE: never exits 1; every error goes through rc.Of
```

### <a id="hb-s6.srf-cu-mcp"></a>srf-cu-mcp

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s6.srf-cu-mcp","claims":[],"code_anchors":["cmd/srf-cu-mcp/main.go::run"],"diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"binary","n":6,"purpose":"srf-cu-mcp is the CU MCP server over stdio JSON-RPC 2.0.","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"srf-cu-mcp"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
`srf-cu-mcp` is the CU MCP server over stdio JSON-RPC 2.0. Usage: `--registry R --cas-dir D [--fake F]... [--scratch-dir S] [--confirm-destructive]`; --mock-gui, --l2-fake and --fake exist only under OS_CU_TEST=1 and --l2-fake needs --deny-list, otherwise E_USAGE, rc 2. `prodGUI` wires the real L2 executor only when prodgate says Prod; `unverified` caps every verdict of a fake executor.

The module doc and flags are in `cmd/srf-cu-mcp/main.go`; its `run` is `cmd/srf-cu-mcp/main.go:30-108 sha_ast:3751a9961c4d`.

```
# PM-P3-HEADER: cmd/srf-cu-mcp/main.go::run
# INPUTS: args []string, the flags; stdin and stdout carry JSON-RPC
# PRECONDITIONS: --registry and --cas-dir are required; test-only flags need OS_CU_TEST=1
# OUTPUTS: int exit code; the JSON-RPC session on stdout
# TIME: dominated by the number of tool calls
# SPACE: O(1) beyond the CAS directory
# ARITHMETIC: integer codes from src/cubook/rc
# FAILURE: missing --registry or --cas-dir, or a test-only flag without OS_CU_TEST=1, returns E_USAGE rc 2; --l2-fake without --deny-list is rc 2
```

### <a id="hb-s6.srf-cu-snap"></a>srf-cu-snap

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s6.srf-cu-snap","claims":[],"code_anchors":["cmd/srf-cu-snap/main.go::run"],"diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"binary","n":6,"purpose":"srf-cu-snap is captures raw RGB24 frames of the capsule framebuffer (or a synthetic one) into a ramws directory and writes path-free evidence JSON.","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"srf-cu-snap"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
`srf-cu-snap` is captures raw RGB24 frames of the capsule framebuffer (or a synthetic one) into a ramws directory and writes path-free evidence JSON. Flags: `--fake-fb|--fb`, `--frames` (1..64, default 1), `--ramws-dir`, `--out`, `--display`, `--iso`, `--app`, `--scene`; exactly one of --fake-fb and --fb, --ramws-dir and --out required, and --fake-fb needs OS_CU_TEST=1. The process sets umask 0o027 first so nothing it creates has world bits.

The module doc and flags are in `cmd/srf-cu-snap/main.go`; its `run` is `cmd/srf-cu-snap/main.go:41-132 sha_ast:8ec1976ba93c`.

```
# PM-P3-HEADER: cmd/srf-cu-snap/main.go::run
# INPUTS: args []string, the flags; stdout and stderr writers
# PRECONDITIONS: --frames in 1..64, --ramws-dir and --out set, exactly one of --fake-fb and --fb
# OUTPUTS: int exit code and path-free evidence JSON (manifest and receipt) under --out
# TIME: O(frames x width x height)
# SPACE: one ring of resident frames in the ramws directory
# ARITHMETIC: integer geometry and byte counts only
# FAILURE: a bad argument is a usage-class error mapped through codeOf; --fake-fb without OS_CU_TEST=1 is refused; the frames never leave the ramws directory
```

### <a id="hb-s6.srf-cu-wlcap"></a>srf-cu-wlcap

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s6.srf-cu-wlcap","claims":[],"code_anchors":["cmd/srf-cu-wlcap/main.go::run"],"diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"binary","n":6,"purpose":"srf-cu-wlcap is the chunked 60 fps capture driver of the CU capsule, pacing an XWD source at 16,666,667 ns [CITED src/cubook/capture/livedriver.go:26].","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"srf-cu-wlcap"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
`srf-cu-wlcap` is the chunked 60 fps capture driver of the CU capsule, pacing an XWD source at 16,666,667 ns [CITED src/cubook/capture/livedriver.go:26]. Flags include `--fake-fb` (needs OS_CU_TEST=1) and `--chunk` (default `capture.DefaultChunkFrames`); `monoNs` is CLOCK_MONOTONIC, the clock whose START t0 the actuator shares; a parse error is E_USAGE rc 2, errors map through rc.Code or rc.Of.

The module doc and flags are in `cmd/srf-cu-wlcap/main.go`; its `run` is `cmd/srf-cu-wlcap/main.go:56-206 sha_ast:cc57db342922`.

```
# PM-P3-HEADER: cmd/srf-cu-wlcap/main.go::run
# INPUTS: args []string, the flags; stdout and stderr writers
# PRECONDITIONS: --fake-fb needs OS_CU_TEST=1; real mode needs the capsule guards
# OUTPUTS: int exit code and the capture evidence of the run
# TIME: paced by the wall clock at one step per FrameNs
# SPACE: bounded by the quota and the ring
# ARITHMETIC: int64 nanoseconds; no float in the pacing path
# FAILURE: a flag parse error is E_USAGE rc 2; E_RAM_NOT_TMPFS and E_RAM_QUOTA keep their own table codes; other errors go through rc.Code or rc.Of
```

## <a id="hb-s7"></a><a id="pm-s7"></a>7. Tests, gates and contracts

<!-- HB:BEGIN hb.section.v2 -->
```json
{"anchor":"hb-s7","claims":[],"code_anchors":[],"diataxis":"reference","hb_id":"HB-OSCUBOOK-001","kind":"oracles","n":7,"purpose":"Which Go tests, gate scripts and conformance suite act as oracles for the cubook packages.","route":{"emit":true,"tags":["software/router"]},"schema":"hb.section.v2","title":"Tests, gates and contracts"}
```
<!-- HB:END -->
**Panel 3 (oracles).** The Go tests per package are the first oracle; the gate script and the contract suite are the second.

| package | Go test functions |
|---|---|
| `src/cubook/rc` | 2 |
| `src/cubook/action` | 5 |
| `src/cubook/ajson` | 4 |
| `src/cubook/prodgate` | 7 |
| `src/cubook/capture` | 115 |
| `src/cubook/exec` | 46 |
| `src/cubook/mcp` | 36 |
| `cmd/srf-cu-exec` | 12 |
| `cmd/srf-cu-mcp` | 11 |
| `cmd/srf-cu-snap` | 12 |
| `cmd/srf-cu-wlcap` | 6 |

The 256 test functions [MEASURED, goextract census over HEAD] are kind `test` in section 8 and are not governed symbols.

- `scripts/cu/gates.sh` runs the Go gates (`capture`, `exec` and the four commands), the `go-cubook-rest` gate for `action`, `ajson`, `mcp`, `prodgate`, `rc`, the `hb-os-tools` gate and the 303 mutants [CITED, scripts/cu/gates.sh].
- `hyperbooks/_tools/os/hb_os_gates.sh HB-OS-cubook` is this book gate: goextract, build, index, live hbtools_v2 gates and the OS gates `g_os_*` (anchor, drift, floor, schema, privacy, rebuild, stdlib).

**Contract schemas (Panel 3, lane K).** One closed JSON Schema file per contract id; `contracts/cubook/run_conformance.py` validates the recorded wire bytes of the four CLIs against them. Every file under `contracts/cubook/schemas/` is cited here.

| contract id | schema file |
|---|---|
| `os.cu.ast/v1` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/os.cu.ast-v1.schema.json` |
| `os.cu.capture-receipt/v1` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/os.cu.capture-receipt-v1.schema.json` |
| `os.cu.mcp-tools/v1` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/os.cu.mcp-tools-v1.schema.json` |
| `os.cu.mcp-wire/v1` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/os.cu.mcp-wire-v1.schema.json` |
| `os.cu.prereg-wl/v2` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/os.cu.prereg-wl-v2.schema.json` |
| `os.cu.prereg-wl/v3` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/os.cu.prereg-wl-v3.schema.json` |
| `os.cu.rc/v1` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/os.cu.rc-v1.schema.json` |
| `os.cu.wl-act-step/v1` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/os.cu.wl-act-step-v1.schema.json` |
| `os.cu.wlcap-bench/v1` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/os.cu.wlcap-bench-v1.schema.json` |
| `os.cu.wlcap-receipt/v1` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/os.cu.wlcap-receipt-v1.schema.json` |
| `os.cu.wlcap-start/v1` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/os.cu.wlcap-start-v1.schema.json` |
| `vqa.act-plan/v1` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/vqa.act-plan-v1.schema.json` |
| `vqa.act-step/v1` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/vqa.act-step-v1.schema.json` |
| `vqa.action/v1` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/vqa.action-v1.schema.json` |
| `vqa.frame/v1` | `contracts/cubook/schemas/vqa.frame-v1.schema.json` |

- Contract conformance (lane K, `contracts/cubook`): `contracts/cubook/run_conformance.py`, `contracts/cubook/tests/test_conformance.py`.

**Recipes.**

1. Verify the book: `taskset -c 10-14 bash hyperbooks/_tools/os/hb_os_gates.sh HB-OS-cubook` (rc 0, 3 book-link, 5 gate failure).
2. After editing Go code: rebuild with `python3 -B hyperbooks/_tools/os/hb_os_driver.py build` so the s8..s10 tables and the manifest follow the code; a code-only or book-only edit alone fails the drift gate.
3. Run a package test: `taskset -c 10-14 go test -p 5 -count=1 ./src/cubook/<pkg>/...` (never the whole Go tree: some packages start an X server).

**Wire schema ids** found as string literals in non-test Go files (first occurrence) and the contract schema file lane K publishes for them.

**Findings (code is truth; no code was changed).**

- F-B01: the doc comment of `ajson.Open` says a second holder gets `E_RAM_LOCKED`-class rc 5, but the code returns the table name `E_GATE`; both are rc 5 (`src/cubook/ajson/ajson.go:27-42 sha_ast:8314ea805cb0`). The book states the code.
- F-B02: `E_STALE`, `STOP` and `INCOMPLETE` have rc 4 or 3 in the table, but only `E_STALE` is produced by `exec.Check`; the book does not claim the others for the executor.
